# Debate Room V3 requirement-to-test ledger

Updated 2026-09-09T10:30:27.925Z. **READY FOR REVIEW, NOT FULL ACCEPTANCE. Local candidate; not deployed.**

All **315** stable IDs are retained: **267** source subrequirements, **44** acceptance IDs and **4** owner directives. Current bounded states: **61 LOCAL_VERIFIED**, **230 PARTIAL**, **24 UNVERIFIED**. There are **zero complete acceptance passes**.

The full original clause, source lines, unchanged planned test ID/procedure, implementation paths, current observations, test-file coverage, evidence references and named gaps are preserved per row in [requirement-inventory.json](requirement-inventory.json). Clauses below are abbreviated only for readability. Source MD/TXT parity and complete classification are recorded in [source-coverage.json](source-coverage.json); that is document coverage, not product acceptance.

`LOCAL_VERIFIED` means a bounded local assertion passed. `PARTIAL` means implementation and some local evidence exist with named remaining checks. `UNVERIFIED` means the required demonstration has not occurred, or a supplied research reference is only classified. None means production-ready. The [verification index](verification-index.md) contains the single latest-candidate suite pointer, historical failures, exact evidence limits and open release gates.

The later owner Study Room policy directive (OWNER-04) supersedes only the original ordinary-room admission default: All users remains default, with Paid/Admin/Admin approval options. Debate Room stays member-neutral. Outage recovery priority and approval/spending boundaries remain in force.

## Event setup, invitations, roster, and readiness

| Requirement ID | Source clause | Current state | Change / files | Test ID / mapped checks | Evidence / observed behavior | Release state | Remaining verification / blocker |
